Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any acoustics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 653. The projective resolution has length at most 166 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to acoustics structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 653/166 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the acoustics structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 693. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 857 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to acoustics theory, we decompose the problem into 693 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
